00064: # Construction wrappers audit finite positive dimensions, endpoint separation
00065: # and per-parent unique include keys before geometry reaches SceneAct.
00066: def attach(parent, key, node):
00067:     assert key not in parent.members, f"Duplicate child key: {key}"
00068:     return parent.include(key, node)
00069: 
00070: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color):
00071:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00072:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00073:     return attach(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00074: 
00075: def bar(parent, key, p, q, radius=.025, color=iron):
00076:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00077:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*p, *q))
00078:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-14
00079:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius=radius, start=p, end=q, color=color))
00080: 
00081: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00082:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00083:     assert len(points) >= 2
00084:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00085:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-14
00086:                for p, q in zip(points, points[1:]))
00087:     return attach(parent, key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00088: 
00089: def ring(parent, key, center, radius, thickness, color, plane="xz", n=48):
00090:     assert radius > 0 and thickness > 0
00091:     x, y, z = center
00092:     points = []
00093:     for k in range(n + 1):
00094:         t = 2 * math.pi * k / n
00095:         if plane == "xz":
00096:             points.append((x + radius*math.cos(t), y, z + radius*math.sin(t)))
00097:         elif plane == "yz":
00098:             points.append((x, y + radius*math.cos(t), z + radius*math.sin(t)))
00099:         else:
00100:             points.append((x + radius*math.cos(t), y + radius*math.sin(t), z))
00101:     return tube(parent, key, points, thickness, color)

00415: # Resolve material targets through their named SceneAct parent subtrees.
00416: # Copy each material slot before changing it, preserving material isolation.
00417: def descendant_meshes(parent):
00418:     meshes = []
00419:     stack = [parent]
00420:     while stack:
00421:         obj = stack.pop()
00422:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00423:             meshes.append(obj)
00424:         stack.extend(obj.children)
00425:     return meshes
00426: 
00427: for key in scene.members:
00428:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(key)
00429:     assert parent is not None, f"Missing realized parent: {key}"
00430:     meshes = descendant_meshes(parent)
00431:     assert meshes, f"Empty realized subtree: {key}"
00432:     for obj in meshes:
00433:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00434:             if slot.material is None:
00435:                 continue
00436:             local_material = slot.material.copy()
00437:             slot.material = local_material
00438:             if local_material.use_nodes:
00439:                 shader = local_material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")

00055: def put(parent, key, node):
00056:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00057:     return parent.include(key, node)

00059: def block(parent, key, size, center, color):
00060:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00061:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00062:     return put(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))

00064: def shaft(parent, key, p, q, radius=.025, color=IRON):
00065:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00066:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*p, *q))
00067:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-12
00068:     return put(parent, key, rod(radius=radius, start=p, end=q, color=color))
00069: 
00070: def hoop(parent, key, center, radius, thickness, color=IRON, plane="xz"):
00071:     assert radius > 0 and thickness > 0
00072:     x, y, z = center
00073:     pts = []
00074:     for i in range(21):
00075:         a = 2*math.pi*i/20
00076:         if plane == "xz":
00077:             pts.append((x+radius*math.cos(a), y, z+radius*math.sin(a)))
00078:         elif plane == "yz":
00079:             pts.append((x, y+radius*math.cos(a), z+radius*math.sin(a)))
00080:         else:
00081:             pts.append((x+radius*math.cos(a), y+radius*math.sin(a), z))
00082:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-12
00083:                for p, q in zip(pts, pts[1:]))
00084:     return put(parent, key, sweep_tube(points=pts, radius=thickness, color=color))

00064: def asset(name):
00065:     a = Asset(name)

00067:     keys[id(a)] = set()
00068:     return a
00069: 
00070: def include(a, name, node):
00071:     assert name not in keys[id(a)]
00072:     keys[id(a)].add(name)
00073:     return a.include(name, node)
00074: 
00075: def finite(p):
00076:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00077: 
00078: def block(a, name, size, loc, col):
00079:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00080:     finite(loc)
00081:     return include(a,name,box(size=size,center=loc,color=col))
00082: 
00083: def bar(a, name, p, q, radius, col):
00084:     finite(p)
00085:     finite(q)
00086:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00087:     assert sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00088:     return include(a,name,rod(radius=radius,start=p,end=q,color=col))
00089: 
00090: def tube(a, name, points, radius, col):
00091:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0 and len(points) >= 2
00092:     for p in points:
00093:         finite(p)
00094:     for p,q in zip(points,points[1:]):
00095:         assert sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00096:     return include(a,name,sweep_tube(points=points,radius=radius,color=col))
00097: 
00098: def sphere(a, name, loc, radius, col):
00099:     finite(loc)
00100:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00101:     return include(a,name,ball(radius=radius,center=loc,color=col))
00102:

00104:     name = names[id(a)]
00105:     assert name not in scene_keys
00106:     scene_keys.add(name)
00107:     scene.include(name,a)
00108:     return a

00016: # Inventory: architecture, workbench, cabinets, wall papers and pliers,
00017: # monitor, scope, power supply, controller, keyboard, calculator, meters,
00018: # lamp, soldering equipment, cans, bottle, PCB, leads, probes and organizer.
00019: # Desktop objects contact z=.80; cabinets and hanging tools sit at the wall.
00020: # Source audit only: no render or image-quality measurement has occurred.
00021: # No fillet boxes or local bpy material overrides are used. Every loop has
00022: # unique per-parent keys; all rod endpoints and consecutive tube points differ.
00023: 
00024: # ---------------- Camera parameters ----------------
00025: CAMERA = (.10, -3.25, 2.90)
00026: TARGET = (0, .20, 1.28)
00027: LENS = 43
00028:

00032: DESK_D = 1.66
00033:

00048: charcoal = (.055, .063, .064)
00049: grey = (.48, .49, .43)
00050: ivory = (.67, .68, .58)

00052: metal = (.38, .40, .38)
00053: red = (.56, .025, .017)

00062: 
00063: scene = Scene("Electronics workbench")
00064: asset_names = {}
00065: scene_keys = set()
00066: child_keys = {}
00067: 
00068: 
00069: def asset(name):
00070:     a = Asset(name)
00071:     asset_names[id(a)] = name
00072:     child_keys[id(a)] = set()
00073:     return a
00074: 
00075: 
00076: def include(a, name, node):
00077:     keys = child_keys[id(a)]
00078:     assert name not in keys, f"Duplicate child key: {name}"
00079:     keys.add(name)
00080:     return a.include(name, node)
00081: 
00082: 
00083: def finite_point(p):
00084:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00085: 
00086: 
00087: def block(a, name, size, loc, col):
00088:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00089:     finite_point(loc)
00090:     return include(a, name, box(size=size, center=loc, color=col))
00091: 
00092: 
00093: def tube(a, name, points, radius, col):
00094:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00095:     assert len(points) >= 2
00096:     for p in points:
00097:         finite_point(p)
00098:     for p, q in zip(points, points[1:]):
00099:         assert sum((p[k] - q[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00100:     return include(a, name, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=col))
00101: 
00102: 
00103: def bar(a, name, p, q, radius, col):
00104:     finite_point(p)
00105:     finite_point(q)
00106:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00107:     assert sum((p[k] - q[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00108:     return include(a, name, rod(radius=radius, start=p, end=q, color=col))
00109: 
00110: 
00111: def sphere(a, name, loc, radius, col):
00112:     finite_point(loc)
00113:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00114:     return include(a, name, ball(radius=radius, center=loc, color=col))
00115: 
00116: 
00117: def add(a, name=None):
00118:     key = name if name is not None else asset_names[id(a)]
00119:     assert key not in scene_keys, f"Duplicate scene key: {key}"
00120:     scene_keys.add(key)
00121:     scene.include(key, a)
00122:     return a
00123:

00143: # ---------------- Rear small-parts cabinets ----------------
00144: for cabinet_i, (cx, width, cols, rows) in enumerate(
00145:         ((.13, .78, 4, 4), (.97, .91, 5, 6))):
00146:     a = asset(f"small parts cabinet {cabinet_i}")
00147:     z0 = 1.70
00148:     height = .67 if cabinet_i == 0 else .91
00149:     block(a, "outer dark frame", (width, .24, height),
00150:           (cx, .77, z0 + height / 2), (.055, .066, .064))
00151:     for r in range(rows):
00152:         for c in range(cols):
00153:             w = (width - .035) / cols
00154:             h = (height - .035) / rows
00155:             x = cx - width / 2 + .018 + (c + .5) * w
00156:             z = z0 + .018 + (r + .5) * h
00157:             color = (.17 + .018 * ((r + c) % 3),
00158:                      .19 + .015 * (r % 2), .17)
00159:             block(a, f"drawer_{r}_{c}", (w - .009, .025, h - .011),
00160:                   (x, .632, z), color)
00161:             block(a, f"label_{r}_{c}", (w * .52, .004, h * .23),
00162:                   (x, .616, z - h * .24), (.61, .64, .56))
00163:             block(a, f"handle_{r}_{c}", (w * .30, .014, .011),
00164:                   (x, .603, z + .007), (.10, .11, .10))
00165:     add(a)

00065: 
00066: def asset(name):
00067:     a = Asset(name)
00068:     names[id(a)] = name
00069:     keys[id(a)] = set()
00070:     return a
00071: 
00072: def include(a, name, node):
00073:     assert name not in keys[id(a)]
00074:     keys[id(a)].add(name)
00075:     return a.include(name, node)
00076: 
00077: def finite(p):
00078:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00079: 
00080: def block(a, name, size, loc, col):
00081:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00082:     finite(loc)
00083:     return include(a, name, box(size=size, center=loc, color=col))
00084: 
00085: def bar(a, name, p, q, radius, col):
00086:     finite(p)
00087:     finite(q)
00088:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00089:     assert sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00090:     return include(a, name, rod(radius=radius, start=p, end=q, color=col))
00091: 
00092: def tube(a, name, points, radius, col):
00093:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0 and len(points) >= 2
00094:     for p in points:
00095:         finite(p)
00096:     for p,q in zip(points,points[1:]):
00097:         assert sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00098:     return include(a,name,sweep_tube(points=points,radius=radius,color=col))
00099: 
00100: def sphere(a,name,loc,radius,col):
00101:     finite(loc)
00102:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00103:     return include(a,name,ball(radius=radius,center=loc,color=col))
00104: 
00105: def add(a):
00106:     name = names[id(a)]
00107:     assert name not in scene_keys
00108:     scene_keys.add(name)
00109:     scene.include(name,a)
00110:     return a

00063: 
00064: def asset(name):
00065:     a = Asset(name)
00066:     names[id(a)] = name
00067:     keys[id(a)] = set()
00068:     return a
00069: 
00070: def include(a, name, node):
00071:     assert name not in keys[id(a)]
00072:     keys[id(a)].add(name)
00073:     return a.include(name, node)
00074: 
00075: def finite(p):
00076:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00077: 
00078: def block(a, name, size, loc, col):
00079:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00080:     finite(loc)
00081:     return include(a,name,box(size=size,center=loc,color=col))
00082: 
00083: def bar(a, name, p, q, radius, col):
00084:     finite(p)
00085:     finite(q)
00086:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00087:     assert sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00088:     return include(a,name,rod(radius=radius,start=p,end=q,color=col))
00089: 
00090: def tube(a, name, points, radius, col):
00091:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0 and len(points) >= 2
00092:     for p in points:
00093:         finite(p)
00094:     for p,q in zip(points,points[1:]):
00095:         assert sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-14
00096:     return include(a,name,sweep_tube(points=points,radius=radius,color=col))
00097: 
00098: def sphere(a, name, loc, radius, col):
00099:     finite(loc)
00100:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00101:     return include(a,name,ball(radius=radius,center=loc,color=col))
00102: 
00103: def add(a):
00104:     name = names[id(a)]
00105:     assert name not in scene_keys
00106:     scene_keys.add(name)
00107:     scene.include(name,a)
00108:     return a

00454: # and unique include slots. No fillets or bpy material overrides are used.
00455: # Composite transformations occur only after all owned children are attached.
00456: realize(scene)

00459: s = bpy.context.scene
00460: world = s.world
00461: if world is None:
00462:     world = bpy.data.worlds.new("Workshop ambient")
00463:     s.world = world
00464: world.use_nodes = True
00465: background = world.node_tree.nodes.get("Background")
00466: if background is None:
00467:     background = world.node_tree.nodes.new("ShaderNodeBackground")
00468:     output = world.node_tree.nodes.get("World Output")
00469:     if output is None:
00470:         output = world.node_tree.nodes.new("ShaderNodeOutputWorld")
00471:     world.node_tree.links.new(background.outputs["Background"],output.inputs["Surface"])
00472: background.inputs["Color"].default_value = (.32,.34,.31,1)
00473: background.inputs["Strength"].default_value = AMBIENT_STRENGTH
